    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

kwargs = {'dbname': 'ecommerce', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '::1': connection timeout expired

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)

        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """

        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)

                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:

                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))

            else:

kwargs = {'dbname': 'postgres', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))

                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))

                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)

            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")

kwargs = {'dbname': 'postgres', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")

        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)

autocommit = False, prepare_threshold = None
context = <psycopg.adapt.AdaptersMap object at 0x0000020F52E71E50>
row_factory = None
cursor_factory = <class 'django.db.backends.postgresql.base.Cursor'>
kwargs = {'dbname': 'postgres', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '::1': connection timeout expired
E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '127.0.0.1': connection timeout expired

cls = <class 'psycopg.Connection'>
conninfo = 'dbname=postgres client_encoding=UTF8 user=ecommerce_user password=change-me host=localhost port=5432 hostaddr=127.0.0.1'
autocommit = False, prepare_threshold = None
context = <psycopg.adapt.AdaptersMap object at 0x0000020F52E71E50>
row_factory = None
cursor_factory = <class 'django.db.backends.postgresql.base.Cursor'>
kwargs = {'dbname': 'postgres', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)
E           django.db.utils.OperationalError: connection timeout expired
E           Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:
E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '::1': connection timeout expired
E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '127.0.0.1': connection timeout expired

                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)

        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.

        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))

kwargs = {'dbname': 'ecommerce', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '::1': connection timeout expired

cls = <class 'psycopg.Connection'>
conninfo = 'dbname=postgres client_encoding=UTF8 user=ecommerce_user password=change-me host=localhost port=5432 hostaddr=127.0.0.1'
autocommit = False, prepare_threshold = None
context = <psycopg.adapt.AdaptersMap object at 0x0000020F52E71E50>
row_factory = None
cursor_factory = <class 'django.db.backends.postgresql.base.Cursor'>
kwargs = {'dbname': 'postgres', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '::1': connection timeout expired
E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '127.0.0.1': connection timeout expired

cursor_factory = <class 'django.db.backends.postgresql.base.Cursor'>
kwargs = {'dbname': 'postgres', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '::1': connection timeout expired

kwargs = {'dbname': 'ecommerce', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

autocommit = False, prepare_threshold = None
context = <psycopg.adapt.AdaptersMap object at 0x0000020F52E71E50>
row_factory = None
cursor_factory = <class 'django.db.backends.postgresql.base.Cursor'>
kwargs = {'dbname': 'ecommerce', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)
E           django.db.utils.OperationalError: connection timeout expired
E           Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:
E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '::1': connection timeout expired
E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '127.0.0.1': connection timeout expired

    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []

            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))

row_factory = None
cursor_factory = <class 'django.db.backends.postgresql.base.Cursor'>
kwargs = {'dbname': 'postgres', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

E           - host: 'localhost', port: '5432', hostaddr: '127.0.0.1': connection timeout expired

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []

            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:

                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)

kwargs = {'dbname': 'ecommerce', 'client_encoding': 'UTF8', 'user': 'ecommerce_user', 'password': 'change-me', ...}

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)

    @classmethod
    def connect(
        cls,
        conninfo: str = "",
        *,
        autocommit: bool = False,
        prepare_threshold: int | None = 5,
        context: AdaptContext | None = None,
        row_factory: RowFactory[Row] | None = None,
        cursor_factory: type[Cursor[Row]] | None = None,
        **kwargs: ConnParam,
    ) -> Self:
        """
        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.
        """
    
        params = cls._get_connection_params(conninfo, **kwargs)
        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")
            lines.extend((f"- {descr}: {error}" for error, descr in conn_errors))
            new_ex = type(last_ex)("\n".join(lines), pgconn=last_ex.pgconn)
>           raise new_ex.with_traceback(None)

                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
    
        if not rv:
            last_ex = conn_errors[-1][0]
            if len(conn_errors) == 1:
                raise last_ex.with_traceback(None)
    
            # Create a new exception with the same type as the last one, containing
            # all attempt errors while preserving backward compatibility.
            lines = [str(last_ex)]
            lines.append("Multiple connection attempts failed. All failures were:")

        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.

        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)

        Connect to a database server and return a new `Connection` instance.

        timeout = timeout_from_conninfo(params)
        rv = None
        attempts = conninfo_attempts(params)
        conn_errors: list[tuple[e.Error, str]] = []
        for attempt in attempts:
            tdescr = (attempt.get("host"), attempt.get("port"), attempt.get("hostaddr"))
            descr = "host: %r, port: %r, hostaddr: %r" % tdescr
            logger.debug("connection attempt: %s", descr)
            try:
                conninfo = make_conninfo("", **attempt)
                gen = cls._connect_gen(conninfo)
                rv = waiting.wait_conn(gen, interval=_WAIT_INTERVAL, timeout=timeout)
            except e._WaitTimeout:
                tex = e.ConnectionTimeout("connection timeout expired")
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(tex))
                conn_errors.append((tex, descr))
            except e.Error as ex:
                logger.debug("connection failed: %s: %s", descr, str(ex))
                conn_errors.append((ex, descr))
            except e._NO_TRACEBACK as ex:
                raise ex.with_traceback(None)
            else:
                logger.debug("connection succeeded: %s", descr)
                break
